- **ma-workbench-json**: Use this skill for M&A deal workbench tasks that require a strict JSON answer from a running TASK_ENV_BASE_URL, answer_template.json, deal IDs, draft terms, playbooks, policy thresholds, consents, employees, material contracts, benchmarks, risk estimates, diligence findings, documents, or notes.
